- **realtime-performance-profiling**: - Orçamento de frame é número, não sensação: 16,6ms a 60fps, 8,3ms a 120fps. - **reasoning-scratchpad-cot**: Use for any task with a checkable right answer that involves arithmetic, multi-step logic, multi-hop lookups, or a plan with dependent steps — think step by step in a separate scratchpad before writing the final answer, instead of jumping straight to a conclusion. - **reasoning-self-consistency**: Use when a task has one objectively checkable correct answer (math, logic, classification, extraction, multiple-choice, deterministic code/calculation output) and a single-pass answer is unreliable — generate several independent reasoning attempts and keep the majority answer instead of trusting the first one. ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-self-consistency) · ★1)

- **reasoning-swarm**: Use when a decision or proposal has genuine multi-stakeholder stakes, spans multiple teams, or carries organization-scale or hard-to-reverse consequences — build-vs-buy calls, architecture decisions affecting several teams, policy with systemic impact, or when the user explicitly wants independent parallel takes stress-tested against each other rather than one recursive pass. Skip for single-claim analysis or solo stress-testing (use reasoning-framework instead), time-pressured decisions, mechanical tasks, or when a recommendation is wanted rather than a tour of considerations.
